The surprise is that the text separates the false “front” from the social body that supports it.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000420/B002","root_001290/B001","root_001512/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_469d08748d24dd4e2857","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:16:1\",\"root_001512\",[\"B003\"]],[\"96:16:2\",\"root_001290\",[\"B001\"]],[\"96:16:3\",\"root_000420\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Perçem aynı zamanda topluluğun önder yüzünü temsil eder; böylece yalan ve suç tek kişiden onu destekleyen bozuk öncü yapıya yayılır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000420/B001","root_001512/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5618601d36a13ead8c21","text":"96:17–18 confirmed the collective reach of `ن ص ي:B003`: the corrupt front calls an assembly and meets a counter-assembly. 96:19 then supplied a bodily correction, contrasting this culpable forelock with a head voluntarily lowered in prostration. The later ayat preserved the distinction between directional error `خ ط ء:B001` and culpable wrongdoing `خ ط ء:B002` rather than collapsing them.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000420/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_64b983b5ccd6f0b2b1f3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:16:3\",\"root_000420\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Yağmurdan mahrum kalan toprak çağrışımı, suçlu perçemi başlangıçtaki merhamet ve öğretimin hayat veren payından yoksun, verimsiz bir ön olarak düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000420/B002","root_001290/B001","root_001512/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_65cafbd74a14ad92f23d","text":"1. **Moral agency concentrated in a seized body-part.** `ن ص ي:B001` supplies forelock and control by taking it; `ك ذ ب:B001` supplies falsehood in speech or action; `خ ط ء:B002` supplies culpable wrongdoing. Grammatically, the two agentive adjectives modify the forelock rather than naming the person separately. The forelock therefore serves as the locus where control, falsification, and guilt become graspable. The packet supplies the metonymy; I infer that seizure targets embodied agency. The fragment reads as a diagnosis of the controller at his point of control.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000420/B001","root_001290/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9042488508715e3dda6e","text":"3. **Intentional falsehood joined to directional failure.** `ك ذ ب:B001` supplies falsehood against truth; `خ ط ء:B001` supplies missing or passing beyond the right direction, while `خ ط ء:B002` preserves culpability. Guidance as a course appears in 96:11 and protective command in 96:12. The two adjectives therefore do different work: one rejects truth, while the other moves off its proper line, and the culpable branch prevents the deviation from being reduced to accident. I infer the directional relation from the earlier guidance construction. The forelock reads as both epistemically false and deliberately misdirected.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000420/B001","root_000420/B002","root_001290/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a787b3533ebe2bafcdff","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:16:2\",\"root_001290\",[\"B001\"]],[\"96:16:3\",\"root_000420\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İki sıfat ayrı işler: biri hakikati çarpıtır, diğeri doğru yönden sapmayı bildirir; suç anlamı bu sapmanın kazara olmadığını korur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000420/B001","root_000420/B003","root_001512/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e095c3633c4ec4e57db1","text":"1. The forelock is grammatically made the bearer of falsehood and sin, so agency is localized at the front. Evidence: `ن ص ي B001` forelock/control, `ك ذ ب B001` falsehood, `خ ط ء B002` culpable sin/wrongdoing. Structural cue: the bare appositional phrase repeats `ناصية` and attaches two feminine adjectives to it. Mechanism: the text transfers the person's denial into the leading bodily sign. Abductive move: the packet gives the body-part branch and moral branches; I infer that the forelock stands for directed agency. Reading change: not “a lying person” only, but “a lying front,” the visible command-point of the person. 2. `خاطئة` preserves both error and deliberate sin. Evidence: `خ ط ء B001` missing/deviating from right, and `خ ط ء B002` culpable wrongdoing. Structural cue: it follows `كاذبة`, so accidental error alone is insufficient, but the deviation branch remains active. Mechanism: falsehood bends direction away from guidance until missing the right path becomes culpable. Abductive move: the packet supplies both non-hit and sin; I infer a layered wrong: misdirected course hardened into blame. Reading change: the forelock is both off-course and guilty. 3. The rain-missed branch makes the forelock a barren front by contrast with the opening teaching/mercy. Evidence: `خ ط ء B003` land missed by rain; `ر ج ع B006` cyclic rain/water; `ك ر م B002` rain-bearing bounty; `ر ح م B001` mercy. Structural cue: the surah's early gift of teaching and mercy contrasts with this dry, false front. Mechanism: a front that turns away from guidance is like land missed by rain: it does not receive what would make it grow. Abductive move: the packet supplies rain-missed and generosity/rain branches; I infer an ecological secondary reading. Reading change: `خاطئة` can also evoke deprivation from life-giving guidance, not just legal guilt.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001512/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fcfb20c2950b308f210b","text":"2. **A corrupt leading front.** `ن ص ي:B003` supplies the elite, leaders, forerunners, and select front. The following `فليدع ناديه` mobilizes a council, while 96:18 mobilizes an opposing force. The branch assigns the forelock a collective role: it is the leading face of a social formation whose moral quality is lying and wrongdoing. I infer that the bodily adjective radiates to the leadership it represents. The line thus exposes not only an individual vice but a corrupt vanguard.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
